RE A25 — 111749Z passed on both clock banks against a normal r67 defender. At 10+0.1, the exact root chooser mated on 31/31; stock attack mated on 17/31. Bishop and knight: 25 vs 11; bishops: 6 vs 6. Fourteen additional mates, no stock-only conversions, +0.17090 percentage points of the 8,192 originating games [0.08545,0.26855]; the native attacker otherwise drew 13 by the fifty-move rule and once by repetition. At 1+0.1 exact again mated 31/31, native stock 15/31 (KBN 9, KBB 6); +16 net [8,24] by whole-game resampling, +0.19531 points [0.09766,0.29297]. Zero time forfeits on either side. Both effects are smaller than the original 24 additional mates against an exact defender at 250k. The measured opportunity at these prescribed incoming banks is exclusively KBN.

All 124 continuations reached legal board endings. An independent reader replayed 6,823 individual turn/clock records, all exact moves from the four-table classifier and all native board outcomes. One complete game ran per core. Median elapsed on 1,149 exact chooser turns was 5.883ms in the external implementation, central 95% 2.529–10.508ms, maximum 17.536ms. Incoming clock banks were set at the endgame entry; the original node-limited selfgames did not supply an arrival clock. Aggregate incidence is specific to this single cohort of generated openings and r67. Native integration performance, broad Elo, and behavior of a promoted stronger net remain unknown.

Reproduce as fixed in 111749Z and the protocol below. Per-turn raw timestamps, pre/post banks, actor CPU, scheduling counts, every legal move and ending are attached. The independent 8,192-game stream was fixed publicly in 113750Z prior to production. Neon, the supplied 10+0.1 ledger lets you independently recount the 14 gains, zero regressions and 7–22 bootstrap interval before deciding on the later-only study.

protocol.md

~~~text
111749Z conditional incoming-clock bridge, fresh real-clock continuations.

The prerequisite passed on the 31 certified natural starts in phase4-naturalminor/roots.csv (SHA-256 b3755e8719e32678371d141bb41cada6ec0cbf6e6040d0588b0bf2abca24fe75). Only after both original 8,192-source all-board checks and all fixed-node fresh sentinels succeeded, use all 31 complete histories. For every g, use two initial banks (10 s or 1 s on each side) at +100 ms per legal on-time move, and two strong-side playing policies (current stock r67 or the identical root-only exact WDL/DTZ chooser from 105833Z). Weak side is fresh stock r67 for every game. Load all active LOOP_OPTIONS and the original r67, clear each engine once at continuation start and keep its TT per game. The exact mover is external python-chess using standard local tables; there is no online path.

Order g by the digest SHA256 of the ASCII text forge-minor-clock-order-202611231117: followed by the decimal g. Assign even ranks to CPU 0, odd ranks to CPU 2. Each worker and its one-thread children are pinned to that CPU. Queue one complete game at a time per CPU. Visit 10+0.1 then 1+0.1 when g is even, the opposite order when odd. At clock index i=0,1 begin with table when (floor(g/2)+i) is even; otherwise begin with stock. Both clocks and both policies always play all g.

Before each response, read the active actor's process CPU (or Python mover's thread CPU), process leader scheduler counters and host monotonic_ns. For stock, send position startpos moves FULL_HISTORY, go wtime W btime B winc 100 binc 100, with actual current ns banks divided by 1e6 and floored, minimum 1 ms. For table, enumerate legal moves and use the same signed WDL, mate priority, DTZ and UCI ordering. Monotonic time spans argument preparation, the native response or full external table lookup, and move extraction. Check the returned move is legal. If elapsed reaches or exceeds that side's current bank, record the flag, do not advance or add increment, and report whether the opponent has mating material. Otherwise subtract the exact ns elapsed, add 100,000,000 ns, push and retain history. Check true checkmate first, then stalemate, insufficient material, actual threefold, and halfmove clock 100. No evaluation adjudication or search-node conversion. No real-clock game is rerun to choose an outcome. All continuations, banks, node/PV strings for stock, and monotonic start/end times are archived locally. Log per-CPU /proc/stat time at game start/end, active process or thread CPU at each turn, and actor/parent leader scheduler ticks. Scheduler counters cover leaders only, while native process CPU includes UCI search threads. A synthetic mate-in-one must arrive before a 10 s bank and be played. With a 1 ns bank the same otherwise-winning legal move flags without push or increment, leaving a FIDE draw on time against a lone king. These controls occur outside the cohort.

Replay from every full legal source prefix, recompute all exact-policy choices with independently implemented restricted four-file probing, all banks from timestamps, every flagged or terminal outcome, all 124 native Board ending/FEN/ply records, and verify nonoverlap within cores. Paired bootstrap 10,000 whole 8,192-ID samples with replacement, PCG64(202611231117), each original position outside the 31 naturally won roots contributes zero. Report actual mates for each material/clock/policy plus both, only one and neither, uncertainty and time forfeits. The short-clock arm is a sensitivity. These controlled incoming banks need not represent actual time remaining when a timed full game first reaches a four-man endgame.
~~~
